- Finding 2, evidence-scope qualification: Celebration treated Overcooked as included released-paper red access even though its captured Mystery Booster Commander Edition printing carries an acorn security stamp. Ordinary-paper protocol explicitly excludes acorn cards.
- Finding 3, grant-versus-payoff qualification: Convoke treated Joyful Stormsculptor as a blue-red explicit grant and used it in the UR combination. Its captured Oracle text only rewards casting a spell that already has convoke; it neither has nor grants convoke.
- Independent bounded crosschecks found no additional defect: all other positive explicit grants/token sources were genuine; other included ordinary-paper records had no acorn/silver/plane/token/scheme/Heroes/playtest leakage; single-face attributed colors matched captured card colors; Convert/Craft and Conjure face handling, Commander ninjutsu/Covercast/Companion combination-only treatment, and the named exclusions/corrections agreed with captured facts. All 43 A/B projected records deep-equal the baseline. Browser execution was deferred because a semantic candidate failure already required a corrected commit.
- Required correction: preserve the original 429 evidence honestly and bind Coven to a separate complete recovered capture; exclude Overcooked from ordinary access; exclude Joyful Stormsculptor as a payoff and remove any now-unsupported UR Convoke combination. Regenerate and submit one new exact candidate for independent re-review.

# Prior handoff - Protocol v1.0 B mechanics

## Control
- Status: `qa-passed` (`qa-failed -> dev-in-progress -> ready-for-qa -> qa-passed`). The three named evidence/metadata findings are repaired; all substantive decisions and player-facing fields are unchanged.
- Branch: `codex/a-mechanics-review`; task baseline `a5a1c6b5463fdff20695aa26e2c6dc05ed4a6487`; branch baseline `9a063b3a98cfcb7672783f83a102e8f968242490`. Reuse the existing preview checkout; no additional branch or worktree.
- Allowed files: `HANDOFF.md`; new `docs/mechanics-research/b-protocol-core.json`, `b-protocol-extended.json`, `B-PROTOCOL-REVIEW.md`; new evidence files under `docs/mechanics-research/b-protocol-evidence/`; `tools/build_mechanics_assessments.py`; `data/mechanics-assessments.v1.json`; `vox-color/mechanical-explorer.html`; `tools/check_assessment_browser.mjs`; `tools/check_supplement_browser.mjs`. Ignored preview package and temporary external research scripts permitted.
- Ownership: two RobDev researchers own disjoint nine-record B packets and uniquely prefixed evidence files; coordinator owns integration/generator/runtime/payload/harness/review table/ledger. Independent RobQA owns ledger only after committed ready-for-qa.

## Grounding and acceptance
- Apply ASSESSMENT-PROTOCOL.md v1.0 and template, plus separate player_copy fields established for A. Local rules/reference material is the starting point. Collect complete keyword/exact-word Scryfall union plus printing history, screen every design, separate multicolor/face/grant and digital/preview/special scopes; capture original narrative sources to HTML/text/readable PDF with hashes when accessible.
- Exactly 18 accepted unmapped B records: Backup, Banding, Bargain, Basic landcycling, Battalion, Battle Cry, Behold, Bestow, Blight, Blitz, Bloodrush, Bloodthirst, Boast, Bolster, Bushido, Buyback, Buy Information, Berzerker. Card-specific flavor labels are not automatically broad mechanics. No filled tiers from counts alone; every color has a defensible tier or explicit unresolved/no-support/incomplete/not-applicable disposition, alternatives, confidence and evidence references.
- Keep all A records and their projected fields unchanged. Add B to the existing assessment producer and runtime using the same symbol/details/evidence flow, allowing per-record evidence dates and route-derived labels. Preserve transport validation and failure isolation. No new app subsystem, dependencies, routes or design theme.
- Player explanations must be intentionally written from evidence, not mechanical substitutions of research prose. Retain meaningful scope/uncertainty/attribution/confidence; no receipts, hashes, protocol/route IDs, audit workflow, or raw status enums in the visible explanations.
- Frozen: 2021 source/runtime identities/content/placements/fixtures/math, existing raw supplemental corpus/scope/example files, existing A source/captures/research/copy/tiers. No publication, push, merge or integration.
- Validation risks handed to RobQA: source/evidence and tier scope, every B record and source link/receipt, A and 2021 parity, deterministic producer, focused all-assessment browser behavior with B unassigned/assigned/card-specific controls and meaningful fail-soft checks. Owner reviews wording/appearance; no broad unrelated wheel suite.

## Independent RobQA — candidate `1bda62107ffd5666bcd28bed42fdf1bbdadca84f`

### Change classification

- QA tier: **QA-4** for 18 new semantic qualification/tier decisions and their shared runtime projection; player-facing explanation is a nested QA-1 seam.
- Changed behavior: adds the complete B assessment batch, its evidence, deterministic projection and focused assessment-surface coverage.

- QA execution: **SEPARATE**, Independent RobQA. The candidate changes protected semantic authority and shared qualification output, so implementation self-review cannot supply the required independence.
- Exact candidate/evidence reference: `1bda62107ffd5666bcd28bed42fdf1bbdadca84f` over task baseline `a5a1c6b5463fdff20695aa26e2c6dc05ed4a6487`; this section is the durable review record.

### Tests selected and objective results

- Candidate scope and whitespace: `git status --short` was empty and `git rev-parse HEAD` returned the exact candidate. `git diff --name-only baseline..candidate` returned 187 paths, all inside the declared envelope. `git diff --check` is clean for authored/material paths outside immutable evidence captures; raw captured HTML and the readable PDF bytes retain source whitespace and make the unfiltered command noisy.
- All-record protocol audit: parsed all 18 records and checked exact W/U/B/R/G dispositions, tier/status/route enums, support/contrary references, population references and totals, special-scope separation, and E3 minimum support. Result: 18 records, zero structural/reference/decision-status errors and no E3 assignment supported by fewer than two qualifying designs.
- Query/screening/chronology audit: independently rebuilt each keyword/exact-term `include:extras` card union from committed pages, compared it with every screened ledger, checked counts, completion/errors and `has_more`, then compared each included card's `first_release` with the minimum eligible committed printing. Result: all 18 unions fully screened; all page/count/completion and first-release checks pass. Two query-parameter metadata mismatches remain below.
- Card-role and tier review: read all 18 candidate ledgers, decision rationales and player explanations, including native versus grant/recipient/interactor/false-positive, face/multicolor attribution, special scopes, nearest alternatives and qualitative Primary/Secondary/Tertiary boundaries. Result: no substantive card-scope or tier defect found. Bargain W Primary is supported by two different native spell roles in a deliberately all-color original rollout; Blight W Secondary is supported by repeated spell/Aura/modal cost upgrades but lacks the repeatable creature-engine role that distinguishes B/R. The distinction is qualitative rather than count-derived.
- Capture/source audit: all source URLs are original external URLs; all capture paths resolve. Bundled `pypdf` reopened 13 referenced PDFs with extractable text and found the cited mechanic term/section in each. SHA-256 was recomputed from **committed Git blobs**, not Windows working-tree bytes: 122 of 134 record capture bindings match; twelve unique shared TXT captures fail, appearing in thirteen record bindings because the Gatecrash capture is reused.
- Deterministic projection: `python tools/build_mechanics_assessments.py --check` exited 0 with `PASS: 43 protocol v1.0 A/B records; deterministic payload and source bindings`.
- A/2021 isolation: recursive JSON comparison found all 25 baseline A payload rows exactly equal in the 43-row candidate. Direct protected-path diff returned `PROTECTED_DIFF_ZERO` for A canonical packets/captures, frozen 2021 owners and existing supplement/scope/example owners.
- Player-language review: read all 18 summaries, 90 per-color explanations and material limitations in order. They name concrete cards/roles, preserve uncertainty and avoid raw receipt/hash/protocol/status language. Final appearance, hierarchy and reading feel remain Owner judgment.

### Findings and verdict

- **MAJOR — committed evidence hashes are not reproducible.** Reproduction: hash each `sources[].capture.files[]` path from `git show 1bda621:<path>`, then compare it with the bound SHA-256. Twelve unique `shared-*.txt` blobs mismatch (thirteen record bindings because `shared-gatecrash.txt` is used by Battalion and Bloodrush). Expected: each recorded byte hash identifies the exact committed capture. Actual: the records bind CRLF working-tree bytes while Git stores LF blobs. Impact: the exact candidate cannot reproduce its claimed source bindings. Narrow repair: normalize those generated transcriptions to LF, bind the committed LF hashes, update `shared-capture-receipts.json`, affected B records and generated payload only.
- **MINOR — two query parameter objects contradict their exact URLs.** Buy Information Q1 and Berzerker Q1 retain `parameters.order: "name"`, while their exact URLs and committed receipts use `order=released`. Expected: normalized parameters describe the saved request. Actual: the duplicated metadata disagrees. Repair only those two parameter values and deterministic derivatives.
- **MINOR — seven archived drafts mislabel summaries as source captures.** `extended-{blitz,bloodrush,bloodthirst,boast,bolster,bushido,buyback}/official-mechanics-source.txt` are unbound agent-written summaries headed `Official source capture`; the actual bound shared captures are valid. Expected: archival notes cannot be mistaken for original evidence. Relabel each retained file as a superseded research note/non-authority and point to its actual shared capture; do not delete it or promote it into `sources`.
- Verdict: **RobQAPass FAIL / `qa-failed`** for this exact candidate. The substantive all-18 decision review is otherwise positive. Return only these named repairs to RobDev; do not reopen tiers, card screening, copy, UI or protected data without new evidence.

### Tests intentionally skipped / proportionality

- Focused Edge assessment harness: not independently rerun after the committed-hash failure made this candidate non-passable. The developer's exact-candidate run covers 43 assessments and six fail-soft cases; the named repairs are evidence/metadata and deterministic-binding changes. Reconsider only if a repair touches projected player content or runtime behavior.
- Broad wheel, 780-query supplement, exhaustive journey, screenshot and viewport matrices: not required; no corresponding protected behavior changed. Owner-visual remains active.
- CPU-heavy validation: **NOT REQUIRED**. No placement engine, score, route or frozen calculation changed.
- Stateful adversarial coverage: **NOT APPLICABLE**. These are immutable semantic records and deterministic payload projection, not a state-ownership/history transition. Focused malformed/missing-payload behavior is already a transport-failure integration case, not Section 13A state ownership.

### Owner review boundary

# Prior handoff - Repeatable supplemental assessment protocol

## Control
- Status: `qa-passed` (qa-failed -> dev-in-progress -> ready-for-qa -> qa-passed after clarifying the one null-semantics sentence). Only the coordinator or Owner may mark complete.
- Branch: `codex/a-mechanics-review`; baseline `82c8add`; total branch baseline `9a063b3a98cfcb7672783f83a102e8f968242490`.
- Exact allowed files: `HANDOFF.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/mechanics-research/ASSESSMENT-PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/mechanics-research/ASSESSMENT-TEMPLATE.json`.
- Scope: write a versioned supplemental research decision protocol, reusable research-record template, and a narrow discoverability/usage pointer in AGENTS. These are research workflow documents; no runtime schema migration, data reassessment, application change, 2021 edit, deployment or publication.

## Acceptance criteria
1. Fixed evidence hierarchy and explicit rule for direct official tiers versus contextual inference, card-derived judgment and user-selected classification; wiki is a locator, Scryfall verifies card facts, counts alone do not assign tiers.
2. Fixed scope, queries, deduplication, card-role/color attribution, chronology, rarity and special-material handling, complete saved-source receipts/PDF practice, and repeatable evidence table.
3. Ordered tier decision rules with a bounded small-sample path, confidence per color, unassigned versus not-applicable/conflicted distinctions, closest-alternative reasoning and no arbitrary scoring.
4. Mandatory player-facing rationale structure, source/decision provenance, versioned revisions and independent review gate; no silently changed criteria between letters or retroactive certification of existing A work.
5. Machine-readable blank template matches the documented protocol and worked cases explain Afflict, Absorb, Annihilator, Affinitycycling and generic rules actions without silently changing existing placements.
- QA: QA-0 documentation/template syntax plus independent consistency review because the document governs future judgment. Check JSON shape and links, compare worked cases with saved records, and Git scope/whitespace. No app/browser/gate sweep is necessary when runtime/data remain unchanged.

## Implementation / QA
- QA repair: replaced ambiguous `None means` with explicit `None of these statuses means the color can never receive the mechanic.` No decision rule, template or other document content changed. Rechecked whitespace and protected implementation diff; both clean.
- Added protocol v1.0, a blank JSON research-record template, and the scoped AGENTS usage pointer. The protocol fixes evidence routes, scope/capture/screening steps, qualitative tier tests, per-color alternatives/confidence/status, small-sample limits, rationale format and revision/review rules. The coordinator authored the protocol and final consistency corrections; the separate RobDev template worker authored only the template and reported JSON/field/whitespace checks.
- Exact implementation envelope remains four files. No application, assessment source/payload, generator, 2021 data or existing evidence changed. Earlier A work requires a bounded conformance review before claiming v1.0 compliance; this task performs no reassessment. In particular, a singleton Colorless example proves access, not a tier without the specified additional support or user decision.
- Developer validation: PowerShell ConvertFrom-Json parses the template; protocol version 1.0 and exact WUBRG decision keys PASS. Both new documents received full content inspection and `git diff --no-index --check -- NUL <file>` with no whitespace defects; tracked `git diff --check` is clean apart from expected line-ending warnings. `git diff --quiet 82c8add -- data tools vox-color` exits 0. Protocol/template cross-links and AGENTS pointer resolve to the two authored files.
- Independent QA-0 consistency review is recorded below. Browser/application tests are intentionally skipped because those files and behavior are unchanged. No automatic protocol validator or importer is claimed. No push, merge, deployment or publication.

- Mode/class/verdict: separate non-implementing Codex Sol-medium reviewer; **QA-4, `qa-failed`** for exact committed candidate `551f3331b6b2c3c0774061cf4ebcd946cdda94de` over baseline `9a063b3a98cfcb7672783f83a102e8f968242490`. Two material acceptance defects are recorded below. QA wrote only `HANDOFF.md`; it made no product/data/source/generator/harness edit, commit, branch operation, research query, publication, push, merge or deployment. Appearance is not accepted or rejected here.
- Candidate and envelope: `git rev-parse HEAD` and the named candidate both resolved to `551f3331b6b2c3c0774061cf4ebcd946cdda94de`; the worktree was clean before QA. `git diff --stat`, `--name-status` and `--check baseline..candidate` showed exactly the ten allowed paths, 76,704 insertions/16 deletions, and no whitespace error. I inspected the producer, generated payload, assessment loader/rendering, package allowlist, focused harness and the small historical-harness expectation changes.
- Required gates: bundled Python `tools/build_mechanics_assessments.py --check` exited 0 with 25 accepted unmapped A records, 70 WUBRG tiers and a byte-identical generated payload. Bundled Python `tools/check_recon.py` exited 0: source, taxonomy and fixture gates PASS and all 53 recon assertions hold. Independent JSON/hash inspection confirmed 25 unique records, 20 derived + two Colorless + three rules classifications, 70 assigned WUBRG tiers, the canonical supplement hash `467835fcfa96c096d4a6c5956465c4043b093c5e4a64ad8b79c5c48a88e8bd0b`, and the four explicit vocabulary-to-catalog-gaps mappings for Abandon, Activate, Actualize and Affinitycycling.
- Protected baseline: `git diff --quiet baseline..candidate` across the frozen 2021 runtime/abilities/fixtures/recon, existing supplement payload/scope, configured 2021 examples, supplemental examples and `docs/mechanics-transfer` exited 0. Base and candidate blobs match for runtime `6081a73537bb6617446e9176b123ae53efe454ef`, abilities `1efe4919b8f92b51d186259a4816084b5a05ce92`, supplement `4ad2f9d4e99c8a271a37724a8fa3a7a61d483d03`, scope `53f203a2f55f59fd98f4339775e6e5d007a18581`, 2021 examples `0f3138b7ec349ecda25622d122d2afa83d2abcc7`, and supplemental examples `dc17671344dd27f03ec34a86998a786b35d70a35`.
- Focused real-browser evidence: bundled Node `tools/check_assessment_browser.mjs --playwright <bundled playwright> --channel msedge` exited 0 against the source checkout and again against the exact stamped `_site` package. It observed 25 assessments/70 tiers, two Colorless classifications, three rules actions and the scoped 2017 Attach note; frozen self-checks 84/84, Article 216, A-Z 543 + Special 44, unchanged math/non-A behavior, keyboard close and 360px containment; atomic missing/bad-tier/wrong-binding/unsafe-URL rejection; delayed refresh/input focus; and escaped source HTML. The first packaged invocation pointed at nonexistent `_site-supplement` and returned ENOENT; correcting the witness to the coordinator's actual `_site` output passed and exposed no product defect. Direct comparison showed packaged explorer/assessment/supplement/scope bytes equal the source and the shell stamped with the exact candidate. Coordinator separately reported package verification of 23 allowlisted files, four inline scripts, 23 HTTP 200 and 24 exclusions 404.
- Syntax and proportionality: bundled Node `--check` exited 0 for both changed browser harnesses; Python AST parsing succeeded for the producer and package script. The existing supplement-browser diff changes only the awaited assessment readiness and the deliberately revised A labels/status assertions; remaining `Rankings unassigned` checks target unassessed Training or dynamically exclude assessed rows. There was no concrete stale-assertion reason to rerun its broad 780-search/358-card sweep; the focused harness covered the changed assessment, failure, math, membership, keyboard and narrow-containment risks. No screenshot regression or broad viewport sweep was run.

### Findings

1. **major — source binding is not checkout-independent, violating deterministic reproduction/source binding acceptance.** Reproduction: with `core.autocrlf=true`, hash each retained research source as working-copy bytes, then hash `git show 551f3331:<path>` bytes. Expected: a source binding derived from the committed semantic JSON is stable in a fresh LF checkout. Actual: all three differ solely by line endings: main assessment working `522bd8f6...1558` vs Git blob `c33cfeaa...5555`; Absorb `7db5bfc7...d46c` vs `02bcdccb...d5a4`; addendum `b4929faf...e1e` vs `4e0c0e45...1c69`. Working files contain 73,188/165/176 CRLF sequences while Git blobs contain zero. Canonical parsed-JSON hashes match across both representations (`ba029427...0013`, `3c21ac0d...5414`, `e530c771...79d`), proving content equality and the narrow repair path. Impact: a fresh Linux/LF checkout regenerates different `sourceBindings` and fails `--check`, so the candidate is not deterministically reproducible from the committed sources.
2. **major — Annihilator loses the required owner-directed classification authority in the generated payload and UI.** Reproduction: open Annihilator in the exact local preview. Expected from acceptance criterion 3 and the retained addendum: identify Primary Colorless / Secondary None / Tertiary None as the Owner/user-directed classification, while describing the supporting rationale as Scryfall-derived; keep Affinitycycling separately provisional/playtest-only. Actual: the panel's accessible label and visible label are `Scryfall-derived assessment - provisional`, it says `Analyst assessment`, and it contains no Owner/user-directed wording. The only owner-directed text remains in the unshipped source addendum, while the producer overwrites that label. Impact: the player-facing page misstates the decision authority and fails the explicit owner-directed/provisional/playtest distinction even though the tiers themselves are correct.

### Rework boundary and Owner checklist

- RobDev repair is limited to (1) canonical parsed-JSON source binding hashes plus regenerated payload/check expectations, and (2) preserving/rendering the Annihilator owner-directed classification label without weakening the separate Affinitycycling provisional/playtest label. Rework QA should rerun only deterministic generation/hash comparison and the focused Annihilator/Affinitycycling browser criterion, plus the required recon check if the candidate changes.
- Owner review is deferred until QA passes. After repair: (1) open Annihilator and confirm the classification is visibly owner-directed while its evidence rationale remains bounded; (2) open Affinitycycling and confirm it remains provisional, low-confidence and playtest-only. No appearance acceptance is inferred.

# Prior handoff — full supplemental scope audit and correction

## Control
- Status: `qa-passed` (`ready-for-qa -> qa-passed`), independent QA on material candidate `2c31303930b88a8c5c1eee4d20b4436cdb6291c2`.
- Base: `8493573b34e0c11bcb04665663364951062feff1`; branch codex/supplemental-mechanic-search reused.
- Owner finding: Affinitycycling was presented too broadly; prior engineering checks proved transfer/rendering but did not establish ordinary-mechanic status. Prior qa-passed scope administratively closed by coordinator `qa-passed -> complete -> idle`, without Owner acceptance of supplemental eligibility. New Owner-authorized audit starts `idle -> dev-in-progress`. All earlier reports remain archived in Git. Prior publication hold persists.
- Outcome: audit all390 accepted records (371 supplemental destinations +19 mappings), distinguish established published/rules support from digital/card-specific/special-format/playtest/designer-only or unresolved evidence, expose distinctions in app and review files.
- Exact allowed repository files: `HANDOFF.md`, `vox-color/mechanical-explorer.html`, `tools/check_supplement_browser.mjs`, `.github/pages/package.py`, `data/mechanics-supplement-scope.v1.json`, `tools/audit_mechanic_scope.mjs`, `docs/mechanics-transfer/SCOPE-AUDIT.md`.
- Allowed external files: existing conversation `outputs/example-review/` inventory/audit JSON and scripts, XLSX and MD review files and builders/validation/previews; backups kept locally. Generated `_site-supplement/` and temporary screenshots only.
- Surfaces: a separate reproducible scope audit/manifest keyed to original source records; explicit runtime packaging/loading; scoped A–Z groups/badges, search badges/reasons and first-visible detail qualifications within one owner; review sheets/Markdown reflect same classification and restrict example suggestions appropriately.
- Non-goals: changing raw transferred evidence or source checkout, frozen2021 source/runtime/examples/tiers/conflicts/math/taxonomy/recon, alias/identity merges, new mechanic prose or rules research/provider queries, new dependencies/routes/detail owners, publishing/push/merge/deployment/hosting.

## Acceptance criteria
1. Every390 record has a disposition with original IDs, evidence-specific basis and precise source scope. Primary inventory and independent review reconcile all records; generic ability/flavor/family rules and provider category do not prove a specific general definition. Unresolved evidence remains explicit. No inferred card legality or mechanics.
2. Playtest and designer-only material is clearly separated in browse/search/details; Affinitycycling cannot appear as an ordinary rules-defined keyword. Official digital, card-specific, Un/special-format and narrow discussion scopes are preserved where supported. 2021 records keep original presentation; mapped supplemental evidence is classified separately.
3. All587 destinations remain discoverable without duplicates or lost exact matches under color lenses. Article remains216. A–Z may be grouped by evidence/scope with alphabetical order inside each group, replacing the prior single undifferentiated alphabet. No supplemental rankings feed math.
4. Runtime classification is local, versioned, fully accounted and bound to unchanged transfer inputs. Missing/malformed/mismatched scope data discloses supplemental-unavailable and keeps2021 usable, never presents unclassified supplemental entries as established.
5. XLSX and Markdown cover all entries and expose eligibility/scope prominently. Existing Owner input is preserved unless it is the explicitly generated13 suggestions; narrow-scope suggestions must not imply approved ordinary examples. Those remain clearly marked review-only or separated from ordinary selection work. Keep distinct identities. Files stay local and do not sync automatically.
6. Required gates, transfer integrity, deterministic audit checks and proportional packaged browser checks pass. Independent QA4 evaluates classifications against evidence, not only producer self-consistency. No publication; exact local candidate plus short Owner checklist.

## Plan / roles
Read-only source inventory and separate evidence critique first; coordinator fixes classification policy and owns producer/data/report/review files. RobDev owns HTML/harness/package after policy is grounded. Independent RobQA after committed ready-for-qa writes HANDOFF only. Available Codex fallback for unavailable prescribed Claude agents.
Baseline/post gate chain and transfer-check; record-by-record audit and independent exception checks; verify fullbrowse/search coverage, first-visible scope labels, mapped2021/conflicts, font/focus/lens/failure safety; package explicitallowlist; workbook inspect/render/recalculate/preservation and MD parity.

## Decisions and limitations
No new research is authorized or needed to distinguish the retained evidence. The audit classifies what the pinned accepted records support, not exhaustive MTG coverage or current tournament legality. Ambiguity is an explicit result, not automatic establishment. Owner's request authorizes clear separation and review-file corrections.
